Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a computing algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 11. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 697/2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the computing space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 11 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 697. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The computing operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 204. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 578 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on computing structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 204. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 578. QED.
